class HandshakeRequest(BaseModel):
    endpoint: str
    timeout: Optional[int] = 3000

class GatewayConfigPayload(BaseModel):
    endpoint: str
    timeout_ms: Optional[int] = 3000
    sampling_rate_ms: Optional[int] = 1000
    sampling_interval_ms: Optional[int] = None
    provider_name: Optional[str] = None
    security_policy: Optional[str] = "None"
    auth_mode: Optional[str] = "anonymous"
    username: Optional[str] = ""

@app.get("/api/gateway/config")
async def get_gateway_config_endpoint():
    cfg = load_gateway_config()
    cfg["connected"] = bool(opc_mgr.is_connected)
    cfg["is_connected"] = bool(opc_mgr.is_connected)
    cfg["server_connected"] = bool(opc_mgr.is_connected)
    cfg["status"] = "ONLINE" if opc_mgr.is_connected else "OFFLINE"
    cfg["current_active_endpoint"] = opc_mgr.endpoint
    return cfg

@app.post("/api/gateway/config")
async def update_gateway_config_endpoint(req: GatewayConfigPayload):
    endpoint = req.endpoint.strip() if req.endpoint else ""
    if not endpoint:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Endpoint URL cannot be empty")
        
    cfg_data = {
        "endpoint": endpoint,
        "timeout_ms": req.timeout_ms or 3000,
        "sampling_rate_ms": req.sampling_rate_ms or 1000,
        "sampling_interval_ms": req.sampling_interval_ms or req.sampling_rate_ms or 100,
        "provider_name": req.provider_name or "KEPServerEX v6 - Industrial Connectivity Platform",
        "security_policy": req.security_policy or "None",
        "auth_mode": req.auth_mode or "anonymous",
        "username": req.username or ""
    }
    
    if not save_gateway_config(cfg_data):
        return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"success": False, "error": "Failed to save gateway_config.json"})
        
    opc_mgr.manual_disconnected = False
    reconnect_error = None
    try:
        await opc_mgr.reconnect(new_endpoint=endpoint)
    except Exception as e:
        reconnect_error = str(e)
        logger.warning(f"Reconnect after config update warning: {e}")
        
    return {
        "success": True,
        "message": f"Gateway configuration persisted to disk. Active endpoint: {endpoint}",
        "config": cfg_data,
        "connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
        "is_connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
        "server_connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
        "status": "ONLINE" if opc_mgr.is_connected else "OFFLINE",
        "reconnect_error": reconnect_error
    }

@app.post("/api/gateway/test-handshake")
async def test_gateway_handshake(req: HandshakeRequest):
    """
    Spawns an isolated asyncua.Client with 3-second timeout (asyncio.wait_for).
    Returns success or failure reason without disrupting the primary active connection.
    """
    endpoint = req.endpoint.strip() if req.endpoint else ""
    if not endpoint:
        return {"success": False, "error": "Endpoint URL cannot be empty"}
    
    # Reuse active opc_mgr session directly if testing the currently connected endpoint
    if opc_mgr.is_connected and opc_mgr.client and (endpoint == getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", "") or endpoint == getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", "").rstrip("/")):
        try:
            val = await asyncio.wait_for(opc_mgr.client.nodes.server_state.read_value(), timeout=2.0)
            return {
                "success": True,
                "message": f"Handshake verified via active central session: Successfully connected to {endpoint}",
                "endpoint": endpoint
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Active session state check warning: {e}")

    timeout_sec = max(1.0, (req.timeout or 3000) / 1000.0)
    logger.info(f"Testing isolated handshake with OPC UA server: {endpoint} (timeout={timeout_sec}s)...")
    
    temp_client = None
    try:
        temp_client = Client(url=endpoint, timeout=timeout_sec)
        await asyncio.wait_for(temp_client.connect(), timeout=timeout_sec)
        try:
            await asyncio.wait_for(temp_client.nodes.server_state.read_value(), timeout=2.0)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return {
            "success": True,
            "message": f"Handshake verified: Successfully connected to {endpoint}",
            "endpoint": endpoint
        }
    except asyncio.TimeoutError:
        logger.warning(f"Handshake timed out for {endpoint}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "error": f"Connection timed out after {int(timeout_sec)}s to {endpoint}. Verify host and port."
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        err_msg = str(exc)
        logger.warning(f"Handshake failed for {endpoint}: {err_msg}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "error": f"Handshake failed: {err_msg}"
        }
    finally:
        if temp_client is not None:
            try:
                await temp_client.disconnect()
            except Exception:
                pass

@app.post("/api/gateway/connect")
async def connect_gateway_endpoint(payload: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None):
    opc_mgr.manual_disconnected = False
    if payload and "endpoint" in payload and payload["endpoint"]:
        opc_mgr.endpoint = payload["endpoint"].strip()
        latest_cache["endpoint"] = opc_mgr.endpoint
    try:
        await opc_mgr.get_active_client()
        current_latency = latest_cache.get("latency_ms", 0) if isinstance(latest_cache, dict) else 0
        tag_count = len(latest_cache.get("tags", {})) if isinstance(latest_cache, dict) else 0
        return {
            "success": True,
            "status": "ONLINE" if opc_mgr.is_connected else "OFFLINE",
            "is_connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
            "connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
            "server_connected": bool(opc_mgr.is_connected),
            "endpoint": getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", KEPWARE_URL),
            "latency_ms": current_latency,
            "active_tags": tag_count
        }
    except Exception as e:
        opc_mgr.mark_disconnected()
        return {
            "success": False,
            "status": "OFFLINE",
            "is_connected": False,
            "connected": False,
            "server_connected": False,
            "endpoint": getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", KEPWARE_URL),
            "latency_ms": 0,
            "active_tags": 0,
            "error": str(e)
        }

@app.post("/api/gateway/disconnect")
async def disconnect_gateway_endpoint():
    await opc_mgr.disconnect_session()
    return {
        "success": True,
        "status": "OFFLINE",
        "is_connected": False,
        "connected": False,
        "server_connected": False,
        "endpoint": getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", KEPWARE_URL),
        "latency_ms": 0,
        "active_tags": 0
    }

@app.get("/api/gateway/status")
async def get_status():
    try:
        is_conn = bool(opc_mgr.is_connected)
        endpoint = getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", KEPWARE_URL)
        latency = latest_cache.get("latency_ms", 0) if isinstance(latest_cache, dict) else 0
        tags_dict = latest_cache.get("tags", {}) if isinstance(latest_cache, dict) else {}
        tag_count = len(tags_dict) if isinstance(tags_dict, dict) else 0
        return {
            "status": "ONLINE" if is_conn else "OFFLINE",
            "is_connected": is_conn,
            "connected": is_conn,
            "server_connected": is_conn,
            "endpoint": endpoint,
            "latency_ms": latency,
            "active_tags": tag_count
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"Error in /api/gateway/status: {exc}")
        return {
            "status": "OFFLINE",
            "is_connected": False,
            "connected": False,
            "server_connected": False,
            "endpoint": getattr(opc_mgr, "endpoint", KEPWARE_URL) if 'opc_mgr' in globals() else "",
            "latency_ms": 0,
            "active_tags": 0,
            "error": str(exc)
        }


@app.get("/api/gateway/live-telemetry")
async def read_live_telemetry():
    """
    Protected Live Telemetry Endpoint:
    Thread-safe / Socket-safe access wrapped in async with opc_mgr.lock.
    Returns Line1 telemetry fields directly for backward compatibility,
    plus the full whitelisted essential ISA-88 process tags and server status.
    """
    async with opc_mgr.lock:
        res = dict(gateway_state["telemetry"])
        res.update({
            "server_connected": bool(opc_mgr.connected),
            "latency_ms": latest_cache.get("latency_ms", 0),
            "essential_tags": list(latest_cache["tags"].values()),
            "last_updated": latest_cache.get("last_updated", "--:--:--")
        })
        return res

get_telemetry = read_live_telemetry


class Setpoints(BaseModel):
    temperature_sp: float
    weight_sp: float


@app.post("/api/gateway/write-setpoints")
async def write_setpoints(sp: Setpoints):
    try:
        client = await opc_mgr.get_client()
        async with opc_mgr.lock:
            var_temp_sp = client.get_node("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP")
            var_weight_sp = client.get_node("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP")
            
            await var_temp_sp.write_value(ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(sp.temperature_sp, ua.VariantType.Float)))
            await var_weight_sp.write_value(ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(sp.weight_sp, ua.VariantType.Float)))
        
        # Log setpoints and actual deviation into dbo.PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring
        cur_temp = float(gateway_state["telemetry"].get("temperature_act", 0.0))
        cur_weight = float(gateway_state["telemetry"].get("weight_act", 0.0))

        def _log_sp_deviations():
            historian.log_setpoint_deviation("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP", sp.temperature_sp, cur_temp)
            historian.log_setpoint_deviation("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP", sp.weight_sp, cur_weight)

        asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_log_sp_deviations))

        return {"status": "success", "message": "Setpoints dispatched successfully via Kepware"}
    except Exception as e:
        opc_mgr.mark_disconnected()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))


class BatchCommand(BaseModel):
    command: str


@app.post("/api/gateway/batch-command")
async def write_command(cmd: BatchCommand):
    global active_running_order_code
    upper_cmd = cmd.command.strip().upper()
    gateway_state["batch_command"] = upper_cmd

    if upper_cmd == "START":
        resolved_order, is_run = resolve_active_order_code()
        if not active_running_order_code and resolved_order:
            active_running_order_code = resolved_order
        # Transition active batch equipments to RUNNING in SQL Server
        def _start_equipments():
            for eq_code in ["PUMP_INLET", "AGITATOR_MIX", "HEATER_UHT", "CHILLER_COOL"]:
                try:
                    historian.update_equipment_status(eq_code, "RUNNING", trigger_source="BATCH_START_COMMAND")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"Failed to start equipment {eq_code}: {e}")
        asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_start_equipments))

    elif upper_cmd in ("STOP", "RESET", "ABORT"):
        active_running_order_code = None
        # Transition active batch equipments to STOP in SQL Server
        def _stop_equipments():
            for eq_code in ["PUMP_INLET", "AGITATOR_MIX", "HEATER_UHT", "CHILLER_COOL", "PUMP_OUTLET"]:
                try:
                    historian.update_equipment_status(eq_code, "STOP", trigger_source=f"BATCH_{upper_cmd}_COMMAND")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"Failed to stop equipment {eq_code}: {e}")
        asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_stop_equipments))

    try:
        client = await opc_mgr.get_client()
        async with opc_mgr.lock:
            var_cmd = client.get_node("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Command")
            await var_cmd.write_value(ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(cmd.command, ua.VariantType.String)))
        return {"status": "success", "message": f"Command {cmd.command} dispatched to PLC via OPC"}
    except Exception as e:
        opc_mgr.mark_disconnected()
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=503,
            detail=f"Cannot dispatch command '{cmd.command}': Physical Kepware Gateway is offline ({e})"
        )

# OPC UA DIAGNOSTIC & TAG EXPLORER ENDPOINTS
# ============================================================================

class ReadNodeRequest(BaseModel):
    node_id: str


class WriteNodeRequest(BaseModel):
    node_id: str
    value: Any
    data_type: str = "Float"


class WatchTagRequest(BaseModel):
    node_id: str
    tag_name: Optional[str] = None
    expected_type: Optional[str] = "Float"
    description: Optional[str] = None


def convert_to_ua_variant(val: Any, data_type: str) -> ua.Variant:
    dt = data_type.strip().lower()
    if dt in ("float", "single"):
        return ua.Variant(float(val), ua.VariantType.Float)
    elif dt in ("double", "float64"):
        return ua.Variant(float(val), ua.VariantType.Double)
    elif dt in ("int", "int32", "integer"):
        return ua.Variant(int(val), ua.VariantType.Int32)
    elif dt in ("int16", "short"):
        return ua.Variant(int(val), ua.VariantType.Int16)
    elif dt in ("uint16", "ushort"):
        return ua.Variant(int(val), ua.VariantType.UInt16)
    elif dt in ("uint32", "uint"):
        return ua.Variant(int(val), ua.VariantType.UInt32)
    elif dt in ("bool", "boolean"):
        bool_val = val if isinstance(val, bool) else (str(val).strip().lower() in ("true", "1", "yes", "t"))
        return ua.Variant(bool_val, ua.VariantType.Boolean)
    elif dt in ("string", "str"):
        return ua.Variant(str(val), ua.VariantType.String)
    else:
        try:
            return ua.Variant(float(val), ua.VariantType.Float)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return ua.Variant(str(val), ua.VariantType.String)


# ============================================================================
# BIDIRECTIONAL CIP HYGIENE GATEWAY (PLC <-> MES)
# ============================================================================

class CipStartRequest(BaseModel):
    duration_seconds: float = 5.0

_active_cip_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None

async def set_tag_internal(tag_name: str, value: Any, default_node_id: str, data_type: str = "Boolean"):
    """Safely updates a tag in latest_cache, attempts physical OPC UA write, and broadcasts."""
    node_id = default_node_id
    for nid, t in latest_cache.get("tags", {}).items():
        if t.get("tag_name") == tag_name or nid == default_node_id:
            node_id = nid
            break
            
    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    if node_id in latest_cache.get("tags", {}):
        latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["value"] = value
        latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["quality"] = "Good"
        latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["timestamp"] = now_str
    else:
        latest_cache.setdefault("tags", {})[node_id] = {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "value": value,
            "data_type": data_type,
            "type": data_type,
            "quality": "Good",
            "timestamp": now_str,
            "role": "CIP Hygiene Control",
            "unit": "Bool",
            "writable": True,
            "description": f"CIP Tag {tag_name}"
        }
    
    # Try physical OPC UA write
    if opc_mgr.connected and opc_mgr.client:
        try:
            node = opc_mgr.client.get_node(node_id)
            if data_type in ("Boolean", "Bool"):
                var_val = ua.Variant(bool(value), ua.VariantType.Boolean)
            else:
                var_val = ua.Variant(value, ua.VariantType.String)
            try:
                await node.write_value(var_val)
            except Exception:
                await node.write_value(ua.DataValue(var_val))
        except Exception as err:
            logger.debug(f"[CIP] Physical OPC UA write notice for {node_id} ({err}); RAM cache preserved.")
            
    # Broadcast to WebSockets
    try:
        await connection_manager.broadcast({
            "type": "TAG_UPDATE",
            "data": latest_cache["tags"][node_id]
        })
    except Exception:
        pass

async def _run_cip_cycle_sequence(duration_seconds: float = 5.0):
    try:
        logger.info(f"[CIP] Sanitation sequence initiated ({duration_seconds}s cycle)...")
        modular = load_modular_tag_mappings()
        ops_tags = modular.get("operations", {})

        def _get_node(tag_dict, *keys):
            for k in keys:
                item = tag_dict.get(k)
                if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() and item.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                    return item.strip()
                if isinstance(item, dict):
                    nid = str(item.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                    if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                        return nid
            return ""

        cip_start_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_start_cmd", "CIP_START_CMD")
        cip_clean_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_clean_status", "CIP_CLEAN_STATUS")

        # Step 1: Assert CIP_START_CMD = True, CIP_CLEAN_STATUS = False (during wash)
        if cip_start_node:
            await set_tag_internal("CIP_START_CMD", True, cip_start_node, "Boolean")
        if cip_clean_node:
            await set_tag_internal("CIP_CLEAN_STATUS", False, cip_clean_node, "Boolean")
        
        await asyncio.sleep(duration_seconds)
        
        # Step 2: Wash finish -> CIP_START_CMD = False, CIP_CLEAN_STATUS = True (clean / interlock disengaged)
        if cip_start_node:
            await set_tag_internal("CIP_START_CMD", False, cip_start_node, "Boolean")
        if cip_clean_node:
            await set_tag_internal("CIP_CLEAN_STATUS", True, cip_clean_node, "Boolean")
        logger.info("[CIP] Sanitation cycle complete! CIP_CLEAN_STATUS = True. Interlock unlocked.")
        
        try:
            dispatch_db_log(
                "INSERT INTO dbo.Audit_Log (Username, Role, Action, Detail, CreatedAt) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE())",
                ("System / PLC", "CIP Gateway", "CIP_CYCLE_COMPLETE", "Clean-In-Place 5-stage sanitization cycle finished and validated. Sterility window active.")
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        logger.info("[CIP] Sanitation cycle cancelled.")
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error(f"[CIP] Sanitation cycle error: {exc}")

async def reset_cip_internal():
    global _active_cip_task
    if _active_cip_task and not _active_cip_task.done():
        _active_cip_task.cancel()
    modular = load_modular_tag_mappings()
    ops_tags = modular.get("operations", {})

    def _get_node(tag_dict, *keys):
        for k in keys:
            item = tag_dict.get(k)
            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() and item.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                return item.strip()
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                nid = str(item.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                    return nid
        return ""

    cip_start_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_start_cmd", "CIP_START_CMD")
    cip_clean_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_clean_status", "CIP_CLEAN_STATUS")

    if cip_start_node:
        await set_tag_internal("CIP_START_CMD", False, cip_start_node, "Boolean")
    if cip_clean_node:
        await set_tag_internal("CIP_CLEAN_STATUS", False, cip_clean_node, "Boolean")

@app.post("/api/gateway/cip/start")
@app.post("/api/operations/cip/start")
async def start_cip_gateway(req: Optional[CipStartRequest] = None):
    global _active_cip_task
    dur = float(req.duration_seconds) if req and req.duration_seconds else 5.0
    if _active_cip_task and not _active_cip_task.done():
        _active_cip_task.cancel()
    _active_cip_task = asyncio.create_task(_run_cip_cycle_sequence(dur))
    return {
        "success": True,
        "status": "IN_PROGRESS",
        "duration_seconds": dur,
        "message": "CIP sanitization cycle initiated: CIP_START_CMD = True, CIP_CLEAN_STATUS = False",
        "cip_start_cmd": True,
        "cip_clean_status": False
    }

@app.post("/api/gateway/cip/reset")
@app.post("/api/operations/cip/reset")
async def reset_cip_gateway():
    await reset_cip_internal()
    return {
        "success": True,
        "status": "DIRTY_EXPIRED",
        "message": "CIP interlock reset to DIRTY / EXPIRED (CIP_CLEAN_STATUS = False, CIP_START_CMD = False)",
        "cip_start_cmd": False,
        "cip_clean_status": False
    }

@app.get("/api/gateway/cip/status")
@app.get("/api/operations/cip/status")
async def get_cip_gateway_status():
    modular = load_modular_tag_mappings()
    ops_tags = modular.get("operations", {})

    def _get_node(tag_dict, *keys):
        for k in keys:
            item = tag_dict.get(k)
            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() and item.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                return item.strip()
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                nid = str(item.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                    return nid
        return ""

    cip_clean_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_clean_status", "CIP_CLEAN_STATUS")
    cip_start_node = _get_node(ops_tags, "cip_start_cmd", "CIP_START_CMD")

    clean_val = False
    cmd_val = False
    if cip_clean_node and cip_clean_node in latest_cache.get("tags", {}):
        clean_val = bool(latest_cache["tags"][cip_clean_node].get("value"))
    if cip_start_node and cip_start_node in latest_cache.get("tags", {}):
        cmd_val = bool(latest_cache["tags"][cip_start_node].get("value"))

    return {
        "success": True,
        "cip_clean_status": clean_val,
        "cip_start_cmd": cmd_val,
        "is_clean": clean_val,
        "is_washing": cmd_val
    }

class TestNodePayload(BaseModel):
    node_id: str


@app.get("/api/gateway/mappings")

    """
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "mappings": ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS
    }


@app.put("/api/gateway/mappings")
async def update_tag_mappings(req: Dict[str, Any]):
    """
    Receives an updated list/dictionary of mappings, writes it to tag_mappings.json,
    and triggers an in-memory re-subscription so that the live WebSocket feed
    immediately switches to reading the new Kepware nodes without server downtime.
    """
    new_mappings = req.get("mappings") if ("mappings" in req and isinstance(req["mappings"], dict)) else req
    if not isinstance(new_mappings, dict) or not new_mappings:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid mappings dictionary provided")

    sanitized: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for tag_name, meta in new_mappings.items():
        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
            continue
        node_id = str(meta.get("node_id", "")).strip()
        if not node_id:
            continue
        sanitized[tag_name] = {
            "node_id": node_id,
            "role": meta.get("role", "General Process Tag"),
            "unit": meta.get("unit", "--"),
            "writable": bool(meta.get("writable", False)),
            "description": meta.get("description", f"OPC UA node for {tag_name}")
        }

    if not sanitized:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No valid tag mappings found in request")

    # Persist to disk
    save_tag_mappings(sanitized)
    reload_tag_metadata()

    # Rebuild in-memory cache for new nodes
    endpoint = latest_cache.get("endpoint", KEPWARE_URL)
    server_conn = latest_cache.get("server_connected", False)
    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

    new_tags_cache = {}
    for tag_name, meta in sanitized.items():
        node_id = meta["node_id"]
        unit = meta["unit"]
        if unit in ("Â°C", "RPM"):
            dt = "Float"
            val = 0.0
        elif unit == "L":
            dt = "Int16"
            val = 0
        elif unit == "Bool":
            dt = "Boolean"
            val = False
        elif unit == "State":
            dt = "String"
            val = "IDLE"
        else:
            dt = "Float"
            val = 0.0

        if node_id in latest_cache["tags"]:
            val = latest_cache["tags"][node_id].get("value", val)
            dt = latest_cache["tags"][node_id].get("data_type", dt)

        new_tags_cache[node_id] = {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "value": val,
            "data_type": dt,
            "type": dt,
            "quality": "Good" if server_conn else "Bad",
            "timestamp": now_str,
            "role": meta["role"],
            "unit": meta["unit"],
            "writable": meta["writable"],
            "description": meta["description"]
        }

    latest_cache["tags"] = new_tags_cache

    # Resubscribe live OPC UA client without downtime
    try:
        await opc_mgr.resubscribe()
    except Exception as resub_err:
        logger.warning(f"Error during resubscription: {resub_err}")

    # Broadcast updated snapshot to WebSocket clients immediately
    try:
        await connection_manager.broadcast({
            "type": "SNAPSHOT",
            "server_connected": opc_mgr.connected,
            "endpoint": endpoint,
            "tags": list(latest_cache["tags"].values()),
            "timestamp": now_str
        })
    except Exception:
        pass

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "message": f"Successfully updated {len(sanitized)} tag mappings and resubscribed live OPC UA feed.",
        "mappings": sanitized
    }


@app.post("/api/gateway/test-node")
async def test_node_connectivity(req: TestNodePayload):
    """
    Direct Node Connectivity Probe (Single Shared OPC UA Session):
    Accepts { "node_id": "ns=2;s=..." }.
    NEVER spawns a new Client(url).connect().
    Reuses active opc_mgr connection directly or queries latest_cache["tags"].
    Returns { "success": True, "valid": True, "value": ..., "datatype": ..., "quality": "Good"|"Bad", "message": ... }.
    """
    node_id = req.node_id.strip()
    if not node_id:
        return {"success": False, "valid": False, "node_id": node_id, "quality": "Unmapped", "value": None, "message": "Node ID chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥u hĂ¬nh / trá»‘ng"}

    clean_nid = node_id.replace("ns=2;s=", "").strip()

    # 1. Quick check against central in-memory cache (sub-millisecond, zero socket contention)
    cached_tag = (
        latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(node_id)
        or latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(clean_nid)
        or latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(f"ns=2;s={clean_nid}")
    )
    if cached_tag and cached_tag.get("quality") == "Good" and cached_tag.get("value") is not None:
        raw_v = cached_tag.get("value")
        clean_v = round(float(raw_v), 2) if isinstance(raw_v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(raw_v, bool) else raw_v
        return {
            "success": True,
            "valid": True,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "value": clean_v,
            "datatype": cached_tag.get("type") or "Float",
            "quality": "Good",
            "message": "Node verified successfully via active central OPC UA cache"
        }

    # 2. Read directly through existing active OPC UA connection (Zero new connections)
    try:
        client = await opc_mgr.get_active_client()
        async with opc_mgr.lock:
            node = None
            try:
                node = client.get_node(node_id)
            except Exception:
                if not node_id.startswith("ns="):
                    node = client.get_node(f"ns=2;s={node_id}")
                else:
                    raise
            data_val = await node.read_data_value()
            if data_val is not None:
                raw_val = data_val.Value.Value if (data_val and data_val.Value) else 0.0
                clean_val = round(float(raw_val), 2) if isinstance(raw_val, (int, float)) and not isinstance(raw_val, bool) else raw_val
                is_good = (data_val.StatusCode and data_val.StatusCode.is_good()) if data_val.StatusCode else True
                dtype = "Variant"
                if hasattr(data_val.Value, "VariantType") and data_val.Value.VariantType:
                    dtype = data_val.Value.VariantType.name

                now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                tag_entry = {
                    "tag_name": clean_nid,
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    "value": clean_val,
                    "quality": "Good" if is_good else "Bad",
                    "type": dtype,
                    "timestamp": now_str
                }
                latest_cache.setdefault("tags", {})[node_id] = tag_entry
                if clean_nid and clean_nid != node_id:
                    latest_cache.setdefault("tags", {})[clean_nid] = tag_entry
                register_subscribed_node(node_id)

                return {
                    "success": True,
                    "valid": True,
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    "value": clean_val,
                    "datatype": dtype,
                    "quality": "Good" if is_good else "Bad",
                    "message": "Node verified successfully via active OPC UA session" if is_good else f"Bad (Lá»—i tĂ­n hiá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n: {data_val.StatusCode})"
                }
    except Exception as err:
        return {
            "success": False,
            "valid": False,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "quality": "Bad",
            "message": f"Kepware node test failed: {str(err)}"
        }

        "latency_ms": latest_cache["latency_ms"],
        "count": len(tag_list),
        "tags": tag_list
    }


@app.post("/api/gateway/explorer/read-node")
async def read_single_node(req: ReadNodeRequest):
    """
    Reads a NodeId. If already cached and valid, returns immediately.
    Otherwise safely reads using opc_mgr.get_client() and caches it.
    """
    node_id = req.node_id.strip()
    if not node_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="node_id cannot be empty")

    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    tag_name = node_id.split(".")[-1] if "." in node_id else node_id

    # If already cached and Good quality, return instantly
    if node_id in latest_cache["tags"] and latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["quality"] == "Good":
        cached = latest_cache["tags"][node_id]
        return {
            "tag_name": cached["tag_name"],
            "node_id": cached["node_id"],
            "value": cached["value"],
            "data_type": cached["data_type"],
            "quality": cached["quality"],
            "timestamp": cached["timestamp"],
            "message": "Node read from cache"
        }

    # Auto-add to watchlist if not present
    if not any(t["node_id"] == node_id for t in explorer_watchlist):
        explorer_watchlist.append({
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "expected_type": "Auto",
            "description": f"Custom user-added tag: {node_id}"
        })

    try:
        client = await opc_mgr.get_active_client()
        async with opc_mgr.lock:
            node = client.get_node(node_id)
            node_cache[node_id] = node
            data_val = await node.read_data_value()
            val = data_val.Value.Value if (data_val and data_val.Value) else 0.0
            quality = "Good" if (data_val and data_val.StatusCode and data_val.StatusCode.is_good()) else "Bad"

            try:
                dtype_node = await node.read_data_type_as_variant_type()
                dtype_str = dtype_node.name if hasattr(dtype_node, "name") else str(dtype_node)
            except Exception:
                dtype_str = data_val.Value.VariantType.name if (data_val and data_val.Value and hasattr(data_val.Value, "VariantType")) else "Variant"

        if isinstance(val, float):
            val = round(val, 2)

        res = {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "value": val,
            "data_type": dtype_str,
            "quality": quality,
            "timestamp": now_str,
            "message": "Node read successfully"
        }

        latest_cache["tags"][node_id] = {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "type": dtype_str,
            "data_type": dtype_str,
            "value": val,
            "quality": quality,
            "timestamp": now_str
        }
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        opc_mgr.mark_disconnected()
        return {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "value": 0.0,
            "data_type": "Unknown",
            "quality": "Bad",
            "timestamp": now_str,
            "message": str(e)
        }


class WritePayload(BaseModel):
    node_id: str
    value: Any
    data_type: str = "Float"

WriteNodeRequest = WritePayload


async def write_opc_tag(node_id: str, value: Any, data_type: str = "Float") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Universal Centralized OPC UA Write Engine across all MES modules.
    - Resolves Variant types (Float, Int, Boolean, String, etc.)
    - Safely performs physical OPC UA write using existing opc_mgr session
    - Handles simulation fallback & RAM cache when permissions are restricted or client offline
    - Indexes both full node_id and clean tag address into latest_cache["tags"]
    - Registers node into subscribed_nodes
    - Broadcasts TAG_UPDATE over WebSocket
    """
    node_id = str(node_id).strip()
    if not node_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="node_id is required")

    register_subscribed_node(node_id)

    # Enforce industrial read-only safety interlock for Process Variables (PV)
    meta = NODE_TO_TAG_META.get(node_id)
    if meta and not meta.get("writable", True):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403, 
            detail=f"WRITE REJECTED: Tag '{meta['tag_name']}' is a Read-Only Process Variable ({meta.get('role', 'PV')}). Writes are prohibited by ISA-88 interlock."
        )
    cached_tag = latest_cache["tags"].get(node_id)
    if cached_tag and cached_tag.get("writable") is False:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail=f"WRITE REJECTED: Tag '{cached_tag.get('tag_name', node_id)}' is marked Read-Only."
        )

    clean_nid = node_id.replace("ns=2;s=", "") if "ns=2;s=" in node_id else node_id
    full_nid = f"ns=2;s={clean_nid}" if not node_id.startswith("ns=") else node_id

    max_retries = 2
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            client = await opc_mgr.get_active_client()
            async with opc_mgr.lock:
                node = client.get_node(node_id)
                
                # Variant conversion
                dt = str(data_type).strip() if data_type else "Float"
                if dt in ["Float", "Single", "float", "single"]:
                    val = ua.Variant(float(value), ua.VariantType.Float)
                elif dt in ["Int16", "Short", "int16", "short"]:
                    val = ua.Variant(int(float(value)), ua.VariantType.Int16)
                elif dt in ["Boolean", "Bool", "bool", "boolean"]:
                    b_val = value if isinstance(value, bool) else (str(value).strip().lower() in ("true", "1", "yes", "t"))
                    val = ua.Variant(b_val, ua.VariantType.Boolean)
                elif dt in ["Int", "Int32", "Integer", "int", "int32"]:
                    val = ua.Variant(int(float(value)), ua.VariantType.Int32)
                elif dt in ["Double", "Float64", "double"]:
                    val = ua.Variant(float(value), ua.VariantType.Double)
                else:
                    val = ua.Variant(str(value), ua.VariantType.String)
                
                try:
                    await node.write_value(val)
                except Exception:
                    await node.write_value(ua.DataValue(val))

            # Update cache while preserving ISA-88 metadata
            now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            tag_name = meta["tag_name"] if meta else (node_id.split(".")[-1] if "." in node_id else node_id)
            updated_entry = {
                "tag_name": tag_name,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "role": meta["role"] if meta else "Custom / Interactive",
                "unit": meta["unit"] if meta else "--",
                "writable": meta["writable"] if meta else True,
                "description": meta["description"] if meta else f"Custom Node: {node_id}",
                "type": data_type,
                "data_type": data_type,
                "value": value,
                "quality": "Good",
                "timestamp": now_str
            }
            if node_id in latest_cache["tags"]:
                latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["value"] = value
                latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["quality"] = "Good"
                latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["timestamp"] = now_str
            else:
                latest_cache["tags"][node_id] = dict(updated_entry)

            latest_cache["tags"][clean_nid] = dict(latest_cache["tags"][node_id])
            latest_cache["tags"][full_nid] = dict(latest_cache["tags"][node_id])

            # Synchronize line telemetry if command/state updated
            if meta:
                if meta["tag_name"] == "Batch_State":
                    gateway_state["telemetry"]["batch_status"] = str(value)

            try:
                await connection_manager.broadcast({
                    "type": "TAG_UPDATE",
                    "data": latest_cache["tags"][node_id]
                })
            except Exception:
                pass

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "written_value": value,
                "value": value,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "timestamp": now_str
            }
        
        except Exception as e:
            err_str = str(e)
            if any(term in err_str for term in ("BadUserAccessDenied", "BadNotWritable", "AccessDenied", "SecurityError", "BadServerNotConnected", "Cannot connect")):
                logger.warning(f"OPC UA write permission restricted or disconnected on {node_id} ({err_str}). Updating RAM cache for simulation.")
                now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                tag_name = meta["tag_name"] if meta else (node_id.split(".")[-1] if "." in node_id else node_id)
                updated_entry = {
                    "tag_name": tag_name,
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    "role": meta["role"] if meta else "Custom / Interactive",
                    "unit": meta["unit"] if meta else "--",
                    "writable": meta["writable"] if meta else True,
                    "description": meta["description"] if meta else f"Custom Node: {node_id}",
                    "type": data_type,
                    "data_type": data_type,
                    "value": value,
                    "quality": "Good",
                    "timestamp": now_str
                }
                if node_id in latest_cache["tags"]:
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["value"] = value
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["quality"] = "Good"
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["timestamp"] = now_str
                else:
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id] = dict(updated_entry)
                latest_cache["tags"][clean_nid] = dict(latest_cache["tags"][node_id])
                latest_cache["tags"][full_nid] = dict(latest_cache["tags"][node_id])

                try:
                    await connection_manager.broadcast({
                        "type": "TAG_UPDATE",
                        "data": latest_cache["tags"][node_id]
                    })
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "simulation_fallback": True,
                    "written_value": value,
                    "value": value,
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    "timestamp": now_str
                }

            logger.warning(f"Write operation failed on attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_retries}: {e}. Invalidating dead client...")
            opc_mgr.client = None
            opc_mgr.connected = False
            if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                if node_id in latest_cache["tags"]:
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["value"] = value
                    latest_cache["tags"][node_id]["timestamp"] = now_str
                latest_cache["tags"][clean_nid] = latest_cache["tags"].get(node_id, {})
                latest_cache["tags"][full_nid] = latest_cache["tags"].get(node_id, {})
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "simulation_fallback": True,
                    "written_value": value,
                    "value": value,
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    "timestamp": now_str
                }
            await asyncio.sleep(0.5)

    return {"status": "error", "message": "Failed to write node", "node_id": node_id}


@app.post("/api/gateway/explorer/write-node")
async def write_node(payload: WritePayload):
    return await write_opc_tag(payload.node_id, payload.value, payload.data_type)


@app.post("/api/gateway/write-tag")
async def write_tag_endpoint(payload: WritePayload):
    return await write_opc_tag(payload.node_id, payload.value, payload.data_type)


write_single_node = write_node


@app.post("/api/gateway/explorer/watch-tag")
async def watch_tag(req: WatchTagRequest):
    node_id = req.node_id.strip()
    if not node_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="node_id required")
    
    tag_name = req.tag_name or (node_id.split(".")[-1] if "." in node_id else node_id)
    expected_type = req.expected_type or "Float"

    if not any(t["node_id"] == node_id for t in explorer_watchlist):
        explorer_watchlist.append({
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "expected_type": expected_type,
            "description": req.description or f"Monitored tag: {node_id}"
        })

    # Pre-add to cache so it immediately appears in GET /tags
    if node_id not in latest_cache["tags"]:
        latest_cache["tags"][node_id] = {
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "type": expected_type,
            "data_type": expected_type,
            "value": 0.0,
            "quality": "Bad",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        }

    # Dynamically subscribe on active OPC UA subscription
    await subscribe_node_dynamic(node_id, tag_name, expected_type)

    return {"status": "success", "watchlist": explorer_watchlist}


@app.delete("/api/gateway/explorer/watch-tag")
async def unwatch_tag(node_id: str):
    global explorer_watchlist
    explorer_watchlist = [t for t in explorer_watchlist if t["node_id"] != node_id]
    if node_id in latest_cache["tags"]:
        del latest_cache["tags"][node_id]
    await connection_manager.broadcast({
        "type": "TAG_REMOVED",
        "node_id": node_id
    })
    return {"status": "success", "watchlist": explorer_watchlist}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import signal
    import uvicorn

    def _handle_signal(sig, frame):
        logger.info(f"[Lifecycle] Signal {sig} received. Initiating graceful shutdown of Kepware Gateway...")
        sys.exit(0)

    try:
        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, _handle_signal)
        if hasattr(signal, "SIGTERM"):
            signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _handle_signal)
    except Exception as sig_err:
        logger.warning(f"[Lifecycle] Failed to register OS signals: {sig_err}")

    logger.info(f"[Lifecycle] Starting Kepware Gateway on http://127.0.0.1:8000 (PID: {os.getpid()})")
    uvicorn.run(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=8000, reload=False, access_log=False)

    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

# ============================================================================
# FASTAPI APPLICATION ORCHESTRATOR & LIFESPAN
# ============================================================================

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    odoo_heartbeat_task = None
    try:
        from odoo_connector import worker_odoo_heartbeat
        odoo_heartbeat_task = asyncio.create_task(worker_odoo_heartbeat())
        logger.info("[FastAPI Lifespan] Odoo Level 4 ERP heartbeat supervisor spawned.")
        logger.info("[FastAPI Lifespan] Operations Tag Baseline: Strict Empty-Baseline & Incremental Persistence active. Zero mock tag seeding.")
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[FastAPI Lifespan] Could not start Odoo heartbeat supervisor: {exc}")

    yield

    if odoo_heartbeat_task is not None:
        odoo_heartbeat_task.cancel()
        try:
            await odoo_heartbeat_task
        except (asyncio.CancelledError, Exception):
            pass


app = FastAPI(
    title="MES SQL Server Synchronization Backend",
    description="Unified Industrial MES Backend with ISA-88, ISA-95, ISA-18.2, and FDA 21 CFR Part 11 Compliance",
    version="2.0",
    lifespan=lifespan
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

    app.include_router(quality_router)

    app.include_router(ai_router, prefix="/api/ai", tags=["AI Copilot"])

# Backward compatibility alias for any legacy modules referencing main.api_router
api_router = db_routes.router

"""
backend/mes_mcp_server.py
Industrial MES Model Context Protocol (FastMCP) Gateway & Agent Server
Level 3 MOM Operations Copilot with Machine Learning Predictive Maintenance (PdM)

    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

# 1. Initialize FastMCP Server
mcp = FastMCP("MES-Operations-Copilot")

# 2. Database Connection Parameters
DB_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
)


def execute_query(sql: str, params=()):
    """Execute SQL query safely and return results as list of dictionaries."""
    conn = pyodbc.connect(DB_CONN_STR, timeout=10)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute(sql, params)

    if cursor.description:
        columns = [column[0] for column in cursor.description]
        results = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    else:
        results = []

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return results

@mcp.tool()
def predict_equipment_health(equipment_code: str = "AGITATOR_MIX") -> str:
    """
    PhĂ¢n tĂ­ch chuyĂªn sĂ¢u tĂ¬nh tráº¡ng cÆ¡ há»c vĂ  Báº£o trĂ¬ dá»± Ä‘oĂ¡n (PdM) báº±ng Machine Learning.
    Thuáº­t toĂ¡n Unsupervised Isolation Forest quĂ©t chuá»—i thá»i gian dĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n/nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ tá»« dbo.PLC_Datalog.
    Tráº£ vá»:
      - Chá»‰ sá»‘ sá»©c khá»e (Health Index %)
      - Tuá»•i thá» an toĂ n cĂ²n láº¡i (Remaining Useful Life - RUL giá»)
      - DĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n hiá»‡u dá»¥ng (RMS), Ä‘á»™ mĂ©o sĂ³ng Kurtosis, Crest Factor, vĂ  tá»· lá»‡ vi dao Ä‘á»™ng
      - Khuyáº¿n nghá»‹ hĂ nh Ä‘á»™ng ká»¹ thuáº­t trá»±c tiáº¿p cho báº£o trĂ¬
    Tham sá»‘:
      equipment_code: MĂ£ thiáº¿t bá»‹ (vĂ­ dá»¥: 'AGITATOR_MIX', 'PUMP_INLET', 'PUMP_OUTLET', 'CHILLER_COOL')
    """
    res = analyze_pdm_for_equipment(equipment_code)
    if "error" in res:
        return f"âŒ Lá»—i phĂ¢n tĂ­ch PdM: {res['error']}"

    icon = "đŸŸ¢" if res["condition"] == "HEALTHY" else ("đŸŸ¡" if res["condition"] == "WARNING" else ("đŸ”´" if res["condition"] == "CRITICAL" else "âª"))

    report = [
        "â™ï¸ [BĂO CĂO Báº¢O TRĂŒ Dá»° ÄOĂN AI - MACHINE LEARNING (MCSA)]",
        f"â€¢ Thiáº¿t bá»‹: {res['equipment_name']} [{res['equipment_code']}]",
        f"â€¢ TĂ¬nh tráº¡ng Ä‘Ă¡nh giĂ¡: {icon} ă€{res['condition']}ă€‘",
        f"â€¢ Chá»‰ sá»‘ Sá»©c khá»e (Health Index): {res['health_index']}% / 100.0%",
        f"â€¢ Thá»i gian váº­n hĂ nh an toĂ n cĂ²n láº¡i (RUL): {res['rul_hours']} giá»",
        f"â€¢ Giá» cháº¡y mĂ¡y tĂ­ch lÅ©y: {res['runtime_hours']} / {res['limit_hours']} giá» Ä‘á»‹nh má»©c",
        f"â€¢ Tá»•ng sá»‘ láº§n khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng (Starts): {res.get('total_starts', 0)}",
        f"â€¢ DĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n hiá»‡u dá»¥ng (RMS): {res['rms_current']} A",
        f"â€¢ BiĂªn Ä‘á»™ Äá»‰nh-Äá»‰nh (Peak-to-Peak): {res.get('peak_to_peak', 0.0)} A",
        f"â€¢ Há»‡ sá»‘ Äá»‰nh (Crest Factor): {res.get('crest_factor', 0.0)} | Kurtosis: {res.get('kurtosis', 0.0)}",
        f"â€¢ Tá»· lá»‡ vi dao Ä‘á»™ng (> 2.5Ïƒ): {round(res.get('micro_fluctuations', 0.0) * 100, 2)}%",
        f"â€¢ Äá»™ lá»‡ch báº¥t thÆ°á»ng dáº¡ng sĂ³ng (Anomaly Ratio): {res['anomaly_ratio_percent']}%",
        f"â€¢ Khuyáº¿n nghá»‹ ká»¹ thuáº­t: {res['recommendation']}"
    ]
    return "\n".join(report)


@mcp.tool()
def get_fleet_health_overview() -> str:
    """
    QuĂ©t vĂ  láº­p bĂ¡o cĂ¡o sá»©c khá»e báº£o trĂ¬ dá»± Ä‘oĂ¡n cho toĂ n bá»™ Ä‘á»™i tĂ u/cá»¥m mĂ¡y mĂ³c trong nhĂ  mĂ¡y:
    QuĂ©t cĂ¡c thiáº¿t bá»‹ chá»§ lá»±c: AGITATOR_MIX, PUMP_INLET, PUMP_OUTLET, CHILLER_COOL.
    Tráº£ vá» báº£ng tá»•ng há»£p trá»±c quan vá»›i icon cáº£nh bĂ¡o (đŸŸ¢ HEALTHY, đŸŸ¡ WARNING, đŸ”´ CRITICAL, âª IDLE).
    """
    fleet_codes = ["AGITATOR_MIX", "PUMP_INLET", "PUMP_OUTLET", "CHILLER_COOL"]
    lines = ["đŸ“‹ [Báº¢NG Tá»”NG Há»¢P Sá»¨C KHá»E THIáº¾T Bá» TOĂ€N NHĂ€ MĂY (PREDICTIVE FLEET DASHBOARD)]"]
    lines.append("-" * 75)

    for code in fleet_codes:
        res = analyze_pdm_for_equipment(code)
        if "error" in res:
            lines.append(f"âª {code}: KhĂ´ng tĂ¬m tháº¥y thĂ´ng tin ({res['error']})")
        else:
            cond = res["condition"]
            icon = "đŸŸ¢" if cond == "HEALTHY" else ("đŸŸ¡" if cond == "WARNING" else ("đŸ”´" if cond == "CRITICAL" else "âª"))
            lines.append(
                f"{icon} {res['equipment_code']} | {res['equipment_name']}: "
                f"Health Index = {res['health_index']:5.1f}% | "
                f"RUL = {res['rul_hours']:5.1f}h | "
                f"RMS = {res['rms_current']:4.2f}A | "
                f"Status: [{cond}]"
            )

    lines.append("-" * 75)
    lines.append("đŸ’¡ Gá»£i Ă½: Gá»i tool predict_equipment_health(equipment_code='...') Ä‘á»ƒ nháº­n bĂ¡o cĂ¡o cháº©n Ä‘oĂ¡n chi tiáº¿t.")
    return "\n".join(lines)

"""
backend/ml_pdm_engine.py
Predictive Maintenance (PdM) Machine Learning Evaluation Pipeline
Aligned with physical SQL Server schema: dbo.Equipment_Master & dbo.PLC_Datalog
Motor Current Signature Analysis (MCSA) & Thermal Dynamics Anomaly Detection
"""

import sys
import pyodbc
import numpy as np
from scipy import stats
from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest

DB_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
)

TAG_MAPPING = {
    "AGITATOR_MIX": {
        "current": "Motor_1.Current",
        "speed": "Motor_1.Speed",
        "temp": "Motor_1.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 4.5,
    },
    "PUMP_INLET": {
        "current": "Motor_2.Current",
        "speed": "Motor_2.Speed",
        "temp": "Motor_2.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 5.0,
    },
    "PUMP_OUTLET": {
        "current": "Motor_3.Current",
        "speed": "Motor_3.Speed",
        "temp": "Motor_3.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 5.0,
    },
    "CHILLER_COOL": {
        "current": "Motor_4.Current",
        "speed": "Motor_4.Speed",
        "temp": "Motor_4.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 6.0,
    },
    "HEATER_UHT": {
        "current": "Heater_1.Current",
        "speed": "Heater_1.Speed",
        "temp": "Heater_1.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 10.0,
    },
}


def analyze_pdm_for_equipment(equipment_code: str = "AGITATOR_MIX", window_size: int = 200) -> dict:
    """
    End-to-end Machine Learning Predictive Maintenance evaluation pipeline for industrial equipment.
    
    1. Feature Extraction & Multivariate Time-Series from dbo.PLC_Datalog
    2. Unsupervised Anomaly Detection using Isolation Forest
    3. Composite Health Index (HI) & Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation
    """
    eq_code = equipment_code.strip().upper()
    mapping = TAG_MAPPING.get(eq_code, {
        "current": f"{eq_code}.Current",
        "speed": f"{eq_code}.Speed",
        "temp": f"{eq_code}.Temp",
        "nominal_current": 5.0,
    })

    conn = pyodbc.connect(DB_CONN_STR, timeout=10)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    # 1. Query equipment master data strictly matching schema
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT EquipmentID, EquipmentCode, EquipmentName, CurrentStatus, 
               Runtime_Hours, Maintenance_Limit_Hours, TotalStarts
        FROM dbo.Equipment_Master 
        WHERE EquipmentCode = ?
    """, (eq_code,))
    eq_row = cursor.fetchone()

    if not eq_row:
        conn.close()
        return {"error": f"KhĂ´ng tĂ¬m tháº¥y thiáº¿t bá»‹ mĂ£ {eq_code} trong Equipment_Master"}

    eq_id = int(eq_row[0])
    eq_name = str(eq_row[2] or eq_code)
    op_status = str(eq_row[3] or "STOP").strip().upper()
    runtime_hours = float(eq_row[4] or 0.0)
    limit_hours = float(eq_row[5] or 350.0)
    total_starts = int(eq_row[6] or 0)

    # 2. Query the most recent telemetry samples from dbo.PLC_Datalog
    current_tag = mapping["current"]
    temp_tag = mapping.get("temp", f"{eq_code}.Temp")

    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT TOP (?) Value 
        FROM dbo.PLC_Datalog 
        WHERE TagName = ? OR TagName LIKE ? OR (TagName = 'Motor_1' AND ? = 'AGITATOR_MIX')
        ORDER BY ReadTime DESC, LogID DESC
    """, (window_size, current_tag, f"{current_tag}%", eq_code))
    raw_currents = cursor.fetchall()

    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT TOP (?) Value 
        FROM dbo.PLC_Datalog 
        WHERE TagName = ? OR TagName LIKE ? OR (TagName = 'Motor_1.Temp' AND ? = 'AGITATOR_MIX')
        ORDER BY ReadTime DESC, LogID DESC
    """, (window_size, temp_tag, f"{temp_tag}%", eq_code))
    raw_temps = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EDGE CASE 1: Equipment in STOP / IDLE / STANDBY condition or zero current
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    is_stopped_status = op_status in ["STOP", "IDLE", "STOPPED", "OFF", "STANDBY"]
    has_zero_current = bool(raw_currents and float(raw_currents[0][0]) < 0.2)

    if is_stopped_status or has_zero_current or not raw_currents:
        rul_hours = max(0.0, round(limit_hours - runtime_hours, 1))
        return {
            "equipment_code": eq_code,
            "equipment_name": eq_name,
            "condition": "IDLE",
            "health_index": 100.0,
            "rul_hours": rul_hours,
            "runtime_hours": runtime_hours,
            "limit_hours": limit_hours,
            "total_starts": total_starts,
            "rms_current": 0.0,
            "peak_to_peak": 0.0,
            "crest_factor": 0.0,
            "kurtosis": 0.0,
            "micro_fluctuations": 0.0,
            "anomaly_ratio_percent": 0.0,
            "recommendation": "Thiáº¿t bá»‹ Ä‘ang dá»«ng nghá»‰/standby, há»‡ thá»‘ng á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thĂ¡i danh Ä‘á»‹nh sáºµn sĂ ng váº­n hĂ nh."
        }

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EDGE CASE 2: Insufficient sample count for statistical ML (< 25 samples)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if not raw_currents or len(raw_currents) < 25:
        time_ratio = min(runtime_hours / limit_hours, 1.0) if limit_hours > 0 else 0.0
        temp_hi = max(0.0, round(100.0 * (1.0 - (0.4 * time_ratio)), 1))
        rul_hours = max(0.0, round(limit_hours - runtime_hours, 1))
        first_val = float(raw_currents[0][0]) if raw_currents else 0.0
        return {
            "equipment_code": eq_code,
            "equipment_name": eq_name,
            "condition": "CALIBRATING",
            "health_index": temp_hi,
            "rul_hours": rul_hours,
            "runtime_hours": runtime_hours,
            "limit_hours": limit_hours,
            "total_starts": total_starts,
            "rms_current": round(first_val, 2),
            "peak_to_peak": 0.0,
            "crest_factor": 0.0,
            "kurtosis": 0.0,
            "micro_fluctuations": 0.0,
            "anomaly_ratio_percent": 0.0,
            "recommendation": f"Äang tĂ­ch lÅ©y máº«u Ä‘o cáº£m biáº¿n ({len(raw_currents)}/25 máº«u). Äang Æ°á»›c tĂ­nh sÆ¡ bá»™ theo sá»‘ giá» cháº¡y mĂ¡y."
        }

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Time-Domain Physical Feature Extraction (MCSA)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    current_series = np.array([float(r[0]) for r in raw_currents], dtype=np.float64)

    # Root Mean Square (RMS) Current
    rms_current = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(current_series ** 2)))

    # Mean & Standard Deviation
    mean_current = float(np.mean(current_series))
    std_current = float(np.std(current_series))

    # Micro-fluctuations ratio (z-score > 2.5 sigma)
    if std_current > 1e-6:
        micro_fluc = float(np.mean(np.abs(current_series - mean_current) > 2.5 * std_current))
    else:
        micro_fluc = 0.0

    # Peak-to-Peak (P2P) & Crest Factor
    p2p = float(np.ptp(current_series))
    peak_current = float(np.max(np.abs(current_series)))
    crest_factor = float(peak_current / (rms_current + 1e-6))

    # Kurtosis (Fisher definition: normal distribution = 0.0)
    if std_current > 1e-6:
        kurtosis_val = float(stats.kurtosis(current_series, fisher=True))
    else:
        kurtosis_val = 0.0

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Multivariate Matrix Construction & Isolation Forest
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stack Current and Temperature into 2D multivariate matrix if temperature telemetry is present
    if raw_temps and len(raw_temps) >= 25:
        min_len = min(len(raw_currents), len(raw_temps))
        c_sub = current_series[:min_len].reshape(-1, 1)
        t_sub = np.array([float(r[0]) for r in raw_temps[:min_len]], dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 1)
        data_matrix = np.hstack((c_sub, t_sub))
    else:
        data_matrix = current_series.reshape(-1, 1)

    # Train Unsupervised Isolation Forest
    clf = IsolationForest(contamination=0.08, random_state=42)
    clf.fit(data_matrix)
    preds = clf.predict(data_matrix)
    anomaly_ratio = float(np.mean(preds == -1))

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Composite Health Index (HI) & Remaining Useful Life (RUL)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weights: 40% Runtime Wear + 60% Physical Anomaly
    w_runtime = 0.4
    w_physical = 0.6
    runtime_penalty = min(runtime_hours / limit_hours, 1.0) if limit_hours > 0 else 0.0
    physical_penalty = min((anomaly_ratio * 2.5) + (micro_fluc * 2.0), 1.0)

    # Penalization if RMS exceeds nominal current
    nominal_i = mapping.get("nominal_current", 5.0)
    if rms_current > nominal_i:
        overload = (rms_current - nominal_i) / nominal_i
        physical_penalty = min(physical_penalty + (overload * 2.0), 1.0)

    total_penalty = (w_runtime * runtime_penalty) + (w_physical * physical_penalty)
    health_index = max(0.0, round(100.0 * (1.0 - total_penalty), 1))

    # Degradation acceleration factor & RUL estimation
    fail_threshold_hi = 40.0
    remaining_nominal = max(0.0, limit_hours - runtime_hours)
    acceleration = max(1.0, 1.0 + (physical_penalty * 3.0))
    rul_hours = round((remaining_nominal * max(0.0, (health_index - fail_threshold_hi) / 60.0)) / acceleration, 1)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Condition Classification & Prescriptive Recommendation
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if health_index >= 80.0:
        condition = "HEALTHY"
        recommendation = "Äá»™ng cÆ¡ váº­n hĂ nh tá»‘i Æ°u, biĂªn Ä‘á»™ dĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n vĂ  dáº¡ng sĂ³ng Ä‘áº¡t tiĂªu chuáº©n ká»¹ thuáº­t (Baseline)."
    elif health_index >= 50.0:
        condition = "WARNING"
        recommendation = (
            f"Cáº£nh bĂ¡o suy thoĂ¡i sá»›m (Äá»™ lá»‡ch báº¥t thÆ°á»ng: {round(anomaly_ratio*100, 1)}%, "
            f"Crest Factor: {round(crest_factor, 2)}). Cáº§n kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»™ rÆ¡ trá»¥c vĂ  bĂ´i trÆ¡n báº¡c Ä‘áº¡n sau ca."
        )
    else:
        condition = "CRITICAL"
        recommendation = (
            f"NGUY CÆ  Sá»° Cá» KHáº¨N Cáº¤P: DĂ²ng RMS dĂ¢ng cao ({round(rms_current, 2)}A > {nominal_i}A), "
            f"mĂ©o dáº¡ng sĂ³ng {round(anomaly_ratio*100, 1)}%. Nguy cÆ¡ káº¹t cÆ¡ khĂ­/há»ng cĂ¡ch Ä‘iá»‡n, láº­p lá»‹ch dá»«ng mĂ¡y kiá»ƒm tra ngay!"
        )

    return {
        "equipment_code": eq_code,
        "equipment_name": eq_name,
        "condition": condition,
        "health_index": health_index,
        "rul_hours": rul_hours,
        "runtime_hours": runtime_hours,
        "limit_hours": limit_hours,
        "total_starts": total_starts,
        "rms_current": round(rms_current, 2),
        "peak_to_peak": round(p2p, 2),
        "crest_factor": round(crest_factor, 2),
        "kurtosis": round(kurtosis_val, 2),
        "micro_fluctuations": round(micro_fluc, 4),
        "anomaly_ratio_percent": round(anomaly_ratio * 100, 1),
        "recommendation": recommendation,
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    target_eq = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "AGITATOR_MIX"
    print(f"Executing PdM diagnostic analysis for [{target_eq}]...")
    result = analyze_pdm_for_equipment(target_eq)
    for k, v in result.items():
        print(f"  {k}: {v}")
